## Narration 347

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A2440)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 14</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا سُلَيْمَانُ بْنُ حَرْبٍ، حَدَّثَنَا حَوْشَبُ بْنُ عَقِيلٍ، عَنْ مَهْدِيٍّ الْهَجَرِيِّ، حَدَّثَنَا عِكْرِمَةُ، قَالَ كُنَّا عِنْدَ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ فِي بَيْتِهِ فَحَدَّثَنَا أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم نَهَى عَنْ صَوْمِ يَوْ��ِ عَرَفَةَ بِعَرَفَةَ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2440]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2440" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated AbuHurayrah: Ikrimah said: We were with AbuHurayrah in his house when he narrated to us: The Messenger of Allah (ﷺ) prohibited fasting on the day of Arafah at Arafah</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2440]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2440" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैरह (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) कहते हैं कि रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने अराफात में यौं-इ-अरफ़ह (नौवीं ज़िल्हज्ज) के रोज़े से मना फ़रमाया.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2440]</p>

## Narration 348

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A2441)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 14</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا الْقَعْنَبِيُّ، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ أَبِي النَّضْرِ، عَنْ عُمَيْرٍ، مَوْلَى عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَبَّاسٍ عَنْ أُمِّ الْفَضْلِ بِنْتِ الْحَارِثِ، أَنَّ نَاسًا، تَمَارَوْا عِنْدَهَا يَوْمَ عَرَفَةَ فِي صَوْمِ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَقَالَ بَعْضُهُمْ هُوَ صَائِمٌ ‏.‏ وَقَالَ بَعْضُهُمْ لَيْسَ بِصَائِمٍ ‏.‏ فَأَرْسَلَتْ إِلَيْهِ بِقَدَحِ لَبَنٍ وَهُوَ وَاقِفٌ عَلَى بَعِيرِهِ بِعَرَفَةَ فَشَرِبَ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2441]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2441" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Umm al-Fadl, daughter of al-Harith, said:On the day of &#x27;Arafah some people near her argued whether the Messenger of Allah (ﷺ) was fasting, some saying that he was, and others saying that he was not. I, therefore, sent him a cup of milk while he was observing the halt at &#x27;Arafah on his camel, and he drank it</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2441]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2441" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   2441 ]</p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">उम्मुल फड़ल बिन्त-इ-हरित (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) से रिवायत है की उनके पास लोग रसूल (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के यौं-इ-अरफ़ह (९थ ज़िल-हज) के रोज़े से मुतालिक झगड़ने लगे वसल्लम) रोज़े से हैं और कुछ कह रहे थे कि रोज़े से नहीं हैं. चुनांचे मैंने दूध का एक प्याला आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) की खिदमत में भेजा उस वक़्त आप अरफ़ह में अपने ऊँट पर वक़ूफ़ किये हुए थे तो आप ने उसे नोश फरमा लिया.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2441]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2441" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ام الفضل بنت حارث رضی اللہ عنہا سے روایت ہے کہ ان کے پاس لوگ رسول صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے یوم عرفہ ( نویں ذی الحجہ ) کے روزے سے متعلق جھگڑنے لگے، کچھ لوگ کہہ رہے تھے کہ آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم روزے سے ہیں اور کچھ کہہ رہے تھے کہ روزے سے نہیں ہیں چنانچہ میں نے دودھ کا ایک پیالہ آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کی خدمت میں بھیجا اس وقت آپ عرفہ میں اپنے اونٹ پر وقوف کئے ہوئے تھے تو آپ نے اسے نوش فرما لیا۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2441]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2441" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Ummul Fadl bint-e-Harith (Radi Allahu Anha) se riwayat hai ki unke paas log Rasool (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke Youm-e-Arafah (9th Zil-Hajj) ke rozay se mutaliq jhagadne lage, kuch log keh rahe the ki Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) rozay se hain aur kuch keh rahe the ki rozay se nahi hain. Chunanche maine doodh ka ek pyala Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ki khidmat mein bheja us waqt Aap Arafah mein apne oont par waqoof kiye hue the to Aap ne use nosh farma liya.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2441]</p>

## Narration 349

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A2442)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 14</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ مَسْلَمَةَ، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ هِشَامِ بْنِ عُرْوَةَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ، - رضى الله عنها - قَالَتْ كَانَ يَوْمُ عَاشُورَاءَ يَوْمًا تَصُومُهُ قُرَيْشٌ فِي الْجَاهِلِيَّةِ وَكَانَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَصُومُهُ فِي الْجَاهِلِيَّةِ فَلَمَّا قَدِمَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم الْمَدِينَةَ صَامَهُ وَأَمَرَ بِصِيَامِهِ فَلَمَّا فُرِضَ رَمَضَانُ كَانَ هُوَ الْفَرِيضَةَ وَتُرِكَ عَاشُورَاءُ فَمَنْ شَاءَ صَامَهُ وَمَنْ شَاءَ تَرَكَهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2442]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2442" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 2442</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   2442 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Aishah said:The Quraish used to fast on the day of &#x27;Ashurah in pre Islamic days. The Messenger of Allah (ﷺ) would fast on it in pre-Islamic period. When the Messenger of Allah (ﷺ) came to Medina, he fasted on it and commanded to fast on it. When the fast of Ramadan was prescribed, that became obligatory, and (fasting on) &#x27;Ashurah was abandoned. He who wishes may fast on it and he who wishes may leave it</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2442]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2442" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 2442</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   2442 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">उम्मुल मोमिनीन आयशा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) कहती हैं कि ज़माना-ए-जाहिलियत में कुरैश आशूरा का रोज़ा रखते थे, और रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) भी इसका रोज़ा नबुवत से पहले रखते थे, फिर जब आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) मदीना आये तो आप ने इसका रोज़ा रखा, और इसके रोज़े का हुक्म दिया, फिर जब रमज़ान के रोज़े फ़र्ज़ हुए तो रमज़ान के रोज़े ही फ़र्ज़ रहे, और आशूरा का रोज़ा छोड़ दिया गया, अब इसे जो चाहे रखे जो चाहे छोड़ दे।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2442]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2442" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ام المؤمنین عائشہ رضی اللہ عنہا کہتی ہیں کہ زمانہ جاہلیت میں قریش عاشورہ کا روزہ رکھتے تھے، اور رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم بھی اس کا روزہ نبوت سے پہلے رکھتے تھے، پھر جب آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم مدینہ آئے تو آپ نے اس کا روزہ رکھا، اور اس کے روزے کا حکم دیا، پھر جب رمضان کے روزے فرض ہوئے تو رمضان کے روزے ہی فرض رہے، اور عاشورہ کا روزہ چھوڑ دیا گیا اب اسے جو چاہے رکھے جو چاہے چھوڑ دے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2442]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2442" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Ummul Momineen Ayesha (Radi Allahu Anha) kehti hain ki zamana-e-jahiliyat mein Quraish Ashura ka roza rakhte the, aur Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) bhi iska roza nabuwat se pehle rakhte the, phir jab Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) Madina aaye to Aap ne iska roza rakha, aur iske rozay ka hukm diya, phir jab Ramadan ke rozay farz hue to Ramadan ke rozay hi farz rahe, aur Ashura ka roza chhod diya gaya, ab ise jo chahe rakhe jo chahe chhod de.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2442]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2442" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 350

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A2443)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 14</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا مُسَدَّدٌ، حَدَّثَنَا يَحْيَى، عَنْ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ، قَالَ أَخْبَرَنِي نَافِعٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ، قَالَ كَانَ عَاشُورَاءُ يَوْمًا نَصُومُهُ فِي الْجَاهِلِيَّةِ فَلَمَّا نَزَلَ رَمَضَانُ قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ هَذَا يَوْمٌ مِنْ أَيَّامِ اللَّهِ فَمَنْ شَاءَ صَامَهُ وَمَنْ شَاءَ تَرَكَهُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2443]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2443" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Ibn &#x27;Umar said:&#x27;Ashurah was a day on which we used to fast in pre-Islamic days. When (fasting of) Ramadan was prescribed, the Messenger of Allah (ﷺ) said: This is one of the days of Allah ; he who wishes may fast on it</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2443]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2443" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हुमा) कहते हैं कि ज़माना-ए-जाहिलियत में हम आशूरा का रोज़ा रखते थे, फिर जब रमज़ान के रोज़ों की फ़र्ज़ियत नाज़िल हुई तो रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: ये (यौम-ए-आशूरा) अल्लाह के दिनों में से एक दिन है, लिहाज़ा जो रोज़ा रखना चाहे रखे, और जो ना चाहे ना रखे।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2443]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2443" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عمر رضی اللہ عنہما کہتے ہیں کہ زمانہ جاہلیت میں ہم عاشورہ کا روزہ رکھتے تھے، پھر جب رمضان کے روزوں فرضیت نازل ہوئی تو رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: یہ ( یوم عاشوراء ) اللہ کے دنوں میں سے ایک دن ہے لہٰذا جو روزہ رکھنا چاہے رکھے، اور جو نہ چاہے نہ رکھے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2443]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2443" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhuma) kehte hain ki zamana-e-jahiliyat mein hum Ashura ka roza rakhte the, phir jab Ramadan ke rozon ki farziyat nazil hui to Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Ye (Youm-e-Ashura) Allah ke dino mein se ek din hai, lehaza jo roza rakhna chahe rakhe, aur jo na chahe na rakhe.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2443]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2443" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>
